# Skills · A · part 85

878 entries, agent-memory-hygiene to agent-observability-dashboard. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **agent-memory-hygiene**: Check filesystem before trusting plugin/skill claims. ([W3JDev/hermes-local](https://github.com/W3JDev/hermes-local/tree/HEAD/profiles/bk-jr/skills/agent-memory-hygiene) · 3 copies)
- **agent-memory-hygiene-review**: Use when memory feels wrong, stale, conflicting, too full, or privacy-sensitive. Keep agent memory small, true as of today and lossless: free space by archiving full text with a pointer left behind, never by deleting, and make corrections visible. ([Richicinschi/awesome-agent-workflows](https://github.com/Richicinschi/awesome-agent-workflows/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/agent-memory-hygiene-review) · ★1)
- **agent-memory-implementation**: Restructures a chaotic or overgrown MEMORY.md into a clean 2-layer architecture based on how Claude Code's autoDream system organizes memory — a lightweight pointer index (always loaded) and topic files (loaded on demand). Stale or superseded memories are deleted or corrected in place — not archived. Use this skill whenever the user says "clean up MEMORY.md", "reorganize my memory files", "MEMORY.md is getting too long", "fix my memory structure", or when you observe that MEMORY.md exceeds 200 lines, contains full paragraphs instead of pointers, or mixes index entries with topic content. ([simbajigege/book2skills](https://github.com/simbajigege/book2skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-memory-implementation) · ★203)
- **agent-memory-improved**: Run a local Agent Memory Service for persistent self-improvement with proper Ed25519 cryptography. Fixed signature implementation for reliable memory storage and retrieval. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/persistent-private-agent-memory) · ★21 · 7 copies)
- **agent-memory-inbox-capture**: Copy the invoking agent's own .claude/agent-memory/&lt;plugin&gt;-&lt;agent&gt;/ entry files that outlive this run into the session's per-branch agent-memory inbox under the scratchpad, so a curator can transfer the durable ones into the repo's own documentation. Takes no arguments and commits nothing. ([TheVoskamps/claude-plugins-marketplace](https://github.com/TheVoskamps/claude-plugins-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/cc-tools/skills/agent-memory-inbox-capture))
- **agent-memory-inbox-cleanup**: Curate the session's per-branch agent-memory inbox in one acting pass. Grades every captured entry transfer-or-delete, writes the durable ones into the repo's own documentation as present-tense constraints, commits and pushes them on the given branch, and empties the inbox. Takes the branch name. ([TheVoskamps/claude-plugins-marketplace](https://github.com/TheVoskamps/claude-plugins-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/cc-tools/skills/agent-memory-inbox-cleanup))
- **agent-memory-integrity**: Audit agent memory for recall-injection flaws. ([zapabob/Skills](https://github.com/zapabob/Skills/tree/HEAD/archive/skill-md-reviewed/sha256/8c/8c0154c494f74ee19c4f31f8c2ce78e141a7b40d024e3fc1c50c00222132df20) · ★2)
- **agent-memory-integrity**: Agent memory systems degrade LLM prefix caching, allow prompt injection via persisted content, and create inconsistent state when live writes update both the store and the in-context representation simultaneously. TRIGGER when: designing a memory system for a persistent agent, debugging prompt cache misses after memory writes, or adding external memory providers to an agent. ([ProsusAI/prism-registry-demo](https://github.com/ProsusAI/prism-registry-demo/tree/HEAD/skills/hermes-agent/agent-memory-integrity))
- **agent-memory-integrity-review**: Review persistent agent memory for poisoning and cross-scope reuse. ([FliorcieLabs/security-agent-skills](https://github.com/FliorcieLabs/security-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-memory-integrity-review))

- **agent-memory-keeper**: AI Agent记忆修复系统。解决每次会话失忆问题——自动复盘对话、提炼关键信息存入长期记忆、下次会话自动加载。 适用于所有OpenClaw Agent，让Agent记住用户偏好、项目进展、技术决策、重要信息。 触发词：记忆、忘了、之前说过、记录一下、帮我记、你还记得 ([Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/community/clawhub/a/agent-memory-keeper) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **agent-memory-keeper**: Carries a coding agent's context across sessions with two files. MEMORY.md holds durable project lessons (verified commands, conventions, decisions, gotchas, user preferences); it is loaded at the start of work and updated whenever the user corrects the agent. HANDOFF.md holds the state of the current task (goal, status checklist, exact next step, failed attempts, open questions, branch and uncommitted files); it is written when a session ends mid-task and checked against git when the next session resumes. Use when the user says "remember this", "don't forget", "forget that note" or "what do… ([milad-ahmd/agent-skills](https://github.com/milad-ahmd/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-memory-keeper) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **agent-memory-keeper**: 当用户说『AI又忘了我之前说的』『记忆越来越乱/重复/互相矛盾』『长期记忆怎么管』『记忆库该清理了』，或要设计/治理一个 agent 的跨会话记忆（笔记/知识库/偏好/项目约定）时使用。把记忆当成『有籍贯、可审计、能收敛』的资产：审计重复·孤儿·陈旧·无溯源四维度，给出收敛(单一真源)+门禁(改前备份)两步流程与可运行脚本（记忆审计器）。理论根基：LGD 三律（有籍·有证·有门禁）。触发词：长期记忆、跨会话记忆、memory governance、记忆治理、记忆重复、记忆冲突、记忆溯源、agent记忆、知识库去重、记忆清理。 ([zhaoxinghua09-cell/agent-skills](https://github.com/zhaoxinghua09-cell/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-memory-keeper) · ★5)

- **agent-memory-kit**: Scaffold and audit a three-layer, cross-agent memory system (identity / discipline / facts) on the user's own machine. Use when the user wants their agent to remember across sessions, asks to set up a memory vault or memory folder, wants to share memory between two agents, or wants an existing memory setup audited for dangling pointers, budget overflow, duplicated layers, or false-green recall. Not for storing secrets, not for building a vector database, and not for note-taking UI work. ([creakzhou/agent-memory-kit](https://github.com/creakzhou/agent-memory-kit/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **agent-memory-layer**: Scalable memory system for AI agents with short-term, long-term, and episodic memory. Covers Redis-backed short-term memory, vector-based long-term memory, and timeline-ordered episodic memory with decay and consolidation. - **agent-memory-loop**: Lightweight self-improvement loop for AI agents. Auto-captures errors, corrections, and discoveries in a fast one-line format. Activation: agent memory, memory forgetting, relevance scoring, memory budget, false memory prevention, adaptive forgetting.
